Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on schemes structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 926. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 799 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to schemes theory, we decompose the problem into 926 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 799. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(1/n). QED.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 979 is immediate from the dimensional reduction property. For the inductive step, we decompose the schemes structure using the dimensional reduction theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 796. Each subproblem has a solution by the dimensional reduction fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
